EMPLOYEES’ STATE INSURANCE 
CORPORATION 


New Delhi, the 20th July 1988 

No. N-15/13/12/1/88-P&D.—In pursuance of po¬ 
wers conferred by Section 46(2) of the Employees’ 
State Insurance Act,’ 1948 (34 of 1948), read with 
Regulation 95-A of the Employees’ State Insurance 
(General) Regulations, 1950, the Director General 
has fixed the 16-7-1988 as the date from which the 
medical benefits as laid down in the said Regulation 
95-A and the Rajasthan Employees’ State Insurance 
(Medical Benefit) Rules, 1955, shall be extended to the 
families of insured persons in the following area in 
the State of Rajasthan namely :— 

“The areas comprising the Revenue village of 

Kherda in tehsil and District Sawaimadhopur”. 

BHAGWATI PRASAD, 
Joint Insurance Commissioner 


• New Delhi, the 18th July 1988 

No. U-16/53/(l)/88-Med. II (Mah.)—In pursuance 
of the resolution passed by ESI Corporation, at its 
meeting held on 25th April, 19S1 conferring upotrthe 
Director General the powers of the Corporation 
under regulation 105 of the ESI (General) Regula¬ 
tions, 1950 and such powers having been further dele¬ 
gated to me vide Director General’s Order No. 1024 
(G) dated 23-5-1983, I hereby authorise Dr. B.R. 
Chougule to function as Medical Authority w.c.f. 
18-7-1988 for one year or till Full-time Medical Re¬ 
feree joins, whichever is earlier for Ichalkaranji area at 
a monthly remuneration in accordance with the exis¬ 
ting norms for the purpose of medical examination of 
insured persons and grant of further certificates to 
them when the correctness of the original certificates 
is in doubt. 

DR. K. M. SAXENA 
Medical Commissioner

New Delhi-110001, the 2nd May 1988 


No. 29-1/85-LI.—Based on the results of the 
actuarial valuation of the assets and liabilities of the 
Post Office Insurance Fund a9 on the 31st March, 
1986, the President is pleased to grant to the holders 
of Postal Life Insurance Policies, which were in force 
on 31st March. 1986 and have continued to be in force 
on 31st March, 1987 a simple reversionary bonus as 
an addition to and payable with the sum assured to 
be allowed for each full month during which the 
policies were in force between the first of April, 
.1985 and the 31st March, 1986 (both dates inclusive) 
•.at the rates given below :—
